     1→import { describe, expect, it } from "bun:test";
     2→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { createTestUser, fetchJson, setupSmokeSuite } from "../smoke-test-kit";
     4→
     5→/**
     6→ * US-5: GitHub commit processor sets task status to done on push to non-main branch.
     7→ * US-6: GitHub commit processor sets task status to completed on merge to main.
     8→ *
     9→ * These tests extend the existing github-webhook smoke test to validate
    10→ * that push events trigger task status transitions based on branch.
    11→ */
    12→
    13→const getRuntime = setupSmokeSuite("webhook-status");
    14→
    15→function makePushEvent(overrides: {
    16→  ref?: string;
    17→  defaultBranch?: string;
    18→  commits?: Array<{
    19→    id: string;
    20→    message: string;
    21→    timestamp: string;
    22→    url: string;
    23→    author: { name: string; email: string; username?: string };
    24→  }>;
    25→}) {
    26→  const defaultBranch = overrides.defaultBranch ?? "main";
    27→  return {
    28→    ref: overrides.ref ?? `refs/heads/${defaultBranch}`,
    29→    repository: {
    30→      full_name: "acme/brain",
    31→      default_branch: defaultBranch,
    32→      html_url: "https://github.com/acme/brain",
    33→    },
    34→    commits: overrides.commits ?? [],
    35→  };
    36→}
    37→
    38→async function pollForTaskStatus(
    39→  surreal: ReturnType<typeof setupSmokeSuite> extends () => infer R ? (R extends { surreal: infer S } ? S : never) : never,
    40→  taskRecord: RecordId<"task", string>,
    41→  expectedStatus: string,
    42→  timeoutMs: number,
    43→): Promise<string | undefined> {
    44→  const start = Date.now();
    45→  while (Date.now() - start < timeoutMs) {
    46→    const [rows] = await surreal
    47→      .query<[Array<{ status: string }>]>(
    48→        "SELECT status FROM $task;",
    49→        { task: taskRecord },
    50→      )
    51→      .collect<[Array<{ status: string }>]>();
    52→    if (rows[0]?.status === expectedStatus) return rows[0].status;
    53→    await Bun.sleep(500);
    54→  }
    55→  // Return whatever status it has now
    56→  const [rows] = await surreal
    57→    .query<[Array<{ status: string }>]>(
    58→      "SELECT status FROM $task;",
    59→      { task: taskRecord },
    60→    )
    61→    .collect<[Array<{ status: string }>]>();
    62→  return rows[0]?.status;
    63→}
    64→
    65→async function setupWorkspaceWithTask(
    66→  baseUrl: string,
    67→  surreal: ReturnType<typeof setupSmokeSuite> extends () => infer R ? (R extends { surreal: infer S } ? S : never) : never,
    68→  suffix: string,
    69→  taskStatus: string,
    70→) {
    71→  const user = await createTestUser(baseUrl, `wh-status-${suffix}`);
    72→
    73→  const workspace = await fetchJson<{ workspaceId: string }>(
    74→    `${baseUrl}/api/workspaces`,
    75→    {
    76→      method: "POST",
    77→      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...user.headers },
    78→      body: JSON.stringify({ name: `Webhook Status ${Date.now()} ${suffix}` }),
    79→    },
    80→  );
    81→
    82→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspace.workspaceId);
    83→  const taskId = `wh-task-${suffix}-${Date.now()}`;
    84→  const taskRecord = new RecordId("task", taskId);
    85→
    86→  await surreal.create(taskRecord).content({
    87→    workspace: workspaceRecord,
    88→    title: `Webhook status test task ${suffix}`,
    89→    status: taskStatus,
    90→    category: "backend",
    91→    priority: "medium",
    92→    created_at: new Date(),
    93→    updated_at: new Date(),
    94→  });
    95→
    96→  return { user, workspace, workspaceRecord, taskId, taskRecord };
    97→}
    98→

     4→
     5→/**

    10→ * and sets matched tasks to done.

    18→  suffix: string,

    20→) {

    22→
    23→  const workspace = await fetchJson<{ workspaceId: string }>(
    24→    `${baseUrl}/api/workspaces`,
    25→    {
    26→      method: "POST",

    28→      body: JSON.stringify({ name: `Commit Check ${Date.now()} ${suffix}` }),
    29→    },
    30→  );
    31→
    32→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspace.workspaceId);
    33→  const taskRecords: Array<{ id: string; record: RecordId<"task", string> }> = [];
    34→
    35→  for (const task of tasks) {

    42→      priority: "medium",
    43→      created_at: new Date(),
    44→      updated_at: new Date(),
    45→    });
    46→    taskRecords.push({ id: task.id, record: taskRecord });

    49→  return { user, workspace, workspaceRecord, taskRecords };

     1→import { describe, expect, it } from "bun:test";
     2→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { createTestUser, fetchJson, setupSmokeSuite } from "../smoke-test-kit";
     4→
     5→/**
     6→ * Backward transitions (unchanged behavior).
     7→ * Server owns abort and reject -- these reset task status to ready.
     8→ *
     9→ * These tests verify that backward transitions continue to work correctly
    10→ * after forward transitions are moved to agents/processors.
    11→ */
    12→
    13→const getRuntime = setupSmokeSuite("backward-transitions");
    14→
    15→async function createSessionWithTask(
    16→  baseUrl: string,
    17→  surreal: ReturnType<typeof setupSmokeSuite> extends () => infer R ? (R extends { surreal: infer S } ? S : never) : never,
    18→  suffix: string,
    19→  taskStatus: string,
    20→) {
    21→  const user = await createTestUser(baseUrl, `backward-${suffix}`);
    22→
    23→  const workspace = await fetchJson<{ workspaceId: string }>(
    24→    `${baseUrl}/api/workspaces`,
    25→    {
    26→      method: "POST",
    27→      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...user.headers },
    28→      body: JSON.stringify({ name: `Backward ${Date.now()} ${suffix}` }),
    29→    },
    30→  );
    31→
    32→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspace.workspaceId);
    33→  const taskId = `backward-task-${suffix}-${Date.now()}`;
    34→  const taskRecord = new RecordId("task", taskId);
    35→
    36→  await surreal.create(taskRecord).content({
    37→    workspace: workspaceRecord,
    38→    title: `Backward transition test ${suffix}`,
    39→    status: taskStatus,
    40→    category: "backend",
    41→    priority: "medium",
    42→    created_at: new Date(),
    43→    updated_at: new Date(),
    44→  });
    45→
    46→  // Create a session linked to the task
    47→  const session = await fetchJson<{ session_id: string }>(
    48→    `${baseUrl}/api/mcp/${workspace.workspaceId}/sessions`,
    49→    {
    50→      method: "POST",
    51→      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...user.headers },
    52→      body: JSON.stringify({ agent: "claude", task_id: taskId }),
    53→    },
    54→  );
    55→
    56→  return { user, workspace, workspaceRecord, taskId, taskRecord, session };
    57→}
    58→
    59→describe("server-owned backward transitions", () => {
    60→  it.skip("Given a session linked to an in_progress task, When the session is aborted, Then the task status resets to 'ready'", async () => {
    61→    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();
    62→    const { user, session, taskRecord } = await createSessionWithTask(
    63→      baseUrl, surreal, "abort-1", "in_progress",
    64→    );
    65→
    66→    const abortResult = await fetchJson<{ aborted: boolean }>(

### SCHEMA VERIFICATION

**`implemented_by` Relation Table**
**Status:** DEFINED
**File:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/schema/surreal-schema.surql`
**Line:** 456-459

**Schema Definition:**
```sql
DEFINE TABLE implemented_by TYPE RELATION IN decision | task OUT git_commit | pull_request SCHEMAFULL;
DEFINE FIELD commit_sha ON implemented_by TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD pr_url ON implemented_by TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD linked_at ON implemented_by TYPE option<datetime>;
```

This relation enables linking decisions and tasks to their corresponding git commits and/or pull requests.

### ROUTE REGISTRATION FILE

**Primary Registration File:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/app/src/server/runtime/start-server.ts`

**Key Locations:**
- Lines 68-359: Route definition object
- Line 22: Import of `wireOrchestratorRoutes` from orchestrator/routes
- Line 53: Import of MCP route handlers via `createMcpRouteHandlers`
- Lines 56-63: Orchestrator route wiring

### ORCHESTRATOR ROUTES IMPLEMENTATION

**File:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/app/src/server/orchestrator/routes.ts`

This file exports `wireOrchestratorRoutes` which:
- Creates orchestrator route handlers with dependency injection
- Validates workspace access via session cookies
- Implements session lifecycle management (createSession, getSessionStatus, abortSession, acceptSession, getReview, rejectSession, sendPrompt)
- Handles SSE stream registration and event iteration
- Integrates with stall detection and assignment validation

### SMOKE TEST INFRASTRUCTURE

**File:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/tests/smoke/smoke-test-kit.ts`

**Key Components:**
1. **setupSmokeSuite(suiteName)** - Factory function that returns smoke runtime getter
   - Creates isolated namespace and database with timestamp-based naming
   - Configures SurrealDB connection (ws://127.0.0.1:8000/rpc by default)
   - Applies full schema from `schema/surreal-schema.surql`
   - Creates test server with OS-assigned port (port: 0)
   - Cleanup: Removes test database and namespace after suite completion

2. **SmokeTestRuntime** - Type definition with:
   - baseUrl: Server URL
   - surreal: SurrealDB connection
   - namespace: Test namespace name
   - database: Test database name
   - port: Assigned server port

3. **Test Infrastructure Functions:**
   - `createTestUser(baseUrl, suffix)` - Creates user via /api/auth/sign-up/email
   - `collectSseEvents<T>(streamUrl, timeoutMs)` - Collects SSE stream events with timeout
   - `fetchJson<T>(url, init)` - Helper for JSON API calls

4. **Lifecycle:**
   - beforeAll: Connects to SurrealDB, creates namespace/database, applies schema, starts server (60s timeout)
   - afterAll: Stops server, cleans up database/namespace (20s timeout, respects SMOKE_KEEP_DB env var)

### SUMMARY

All requested endpoints are implemented:
- GitHub webhook endpoint: IMPLEMENTED
- Commit check endpoints (pre-check, post-check): IMPLEMENTED
- MCP session endpoints (start, end): IMPLEMENTED
- Orchestrator session endpoints (accept, abort, reject, assign, status, review, prompt, stream): IMPLEMENTED
- `implemented_by` relation table: DEFINED in schema

The route registration architecture uses a clean factory pattern with dependency injection through the `wireOrchestratorRoutes` and `createMcpRouteHandlers` functions, both of which are wired in `start-server.ts`.

Perfect! I now have all the information needed. Let me provide you with the exact URL patterns for all three categories of endpoints:

## Exact URL Patterns Found

### 1. Commit Check Endpoints
Located in `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/app/src/server/runtime/start-server.ts` (lines 321-335):

- **Pre-check:** `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/commits/pre-check`
- **Post-check:** `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/commits/post-check`

Both use the MCP route handler `mcpHandlers.handlePreCheck()` and `mcpHandlers.handlePostCheck()` respectively.

### 2. Session Creation Endpoint
Located in `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/app/src/server/runtime/start-server.ts` (lines 311-315):

- **Session start:** `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/sessions/start`
- **Session end:** `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/sessions/end`

Both use MCP route handlers `mcpHandlers.handleSessionStart()` and `mcpHandlers.handleSessionEnd()` respectively.

### 3. Orchestrator Accept/Abort/Reject Endpoints
Located in `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/app/src/server/runtime/start-server.ts` (lines 170-190):

**All orchestrator endpoints include `workspaceId` in the URL path:**

- **Accept:** `POST /api/orchestrator/:workspaceId/sessions/:sessionId/accept`
- **Abort:** `POST /api/orchestrator/:workspaceId/sessions/:sessionId/abort`
- **Reject:** `POST /api/orchestrator/:workspaceId/sessions/:sessionId/reject`
- **Status:** `GET /api/orchestrator/:workspaceId/sessions/:sessionId`
- **Review:** `GET /api/orchestrator/:workspaceId/sessions/:sessionId/review`
- **Prompt:** `POST /api/orchestrator/:workspaceId/sessions/:sessionId/prompt`
- **Stream:** `GET /api/orchestrator/:workspaceId/sessions/:sessionId/stream`
- **Assign:** `POST /api/orchestrator/:workspaceId/assign`

The implementation confirms that all orchestrator endpoints require `workspaceId` as a path parameter and validate workspace membership before executing the request (see `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/app/src/server/orchestrator/routes.ts`, lines 310-326).

Perfect! Now I have all the information needed. Let me compile my findings:

## Summary

I've found and analyzed the MCP route handlers and orchestrator handlers. Here's what you asked for:

---

### 1. **`handleSessionStart`** (MCP route: `/api/mcp/:workspaceId/sessions`)

**Request body:**
```typescript
{
  agent: string;              // required
  project_id?: string;        // optional, raw UUID (no table: prefix)
  task_id?: string;          // optional, raw UUID (no table: prefix)
}
```

**Response:**
```typescript
{
  session_id: string;        // raw UUID
}
```
Returns HTTP 201 (Created).

**Key details:**
- Requires `session:write` scope
- Validates that project_id and task_id (if provided) are scoped to the workspace
- Calls `createAgentSession()` which creates an `agent_session` record and optionally links it to a task

---

### 2. **`handlePreCheck`** (MCP route: `/api/mcp/:workspaceId/commits/pre-check`)

**Request body:**
```typescript
{
  project_id?: string;       // optional, raw UUID
  diff: string;              // required, git diff
  commit_message: string;    // required
}
```

**Response:**
```typescript
{
  task_completions: Array<{
    task_title: string;
    confidence: number;      // 0-1 score
  }>;
  unlogged_decisions: Array<{
    description: string;
  }>;
  constraint_violations: Array<{
    constraint: string;
    violation: string;
    severity: "warning" | "error";
  }>;
  summary: string;
}
```
Returns HTTP 200.

**Key details:**
- Requires `graph:reason` scope
- Runs LLM analysis (generateObject with commitCheckSchema)
- Loads workspace decisions, constraints, and active tasks for context
- Truncates diff to 8000 chars for token budget
- Does NOT update task status — that's post-check's job

---

### 3. **`handlePostCheck`** (MCP route: `/api/mcp/:workspaceId/commits/post-check`)

**Request body:**
```typescript
{
  message?: string;          // commit message (alias: commit_message)
  commit_message?: string;
}
```
(At least one of `message` or `commit_message` is required)

**Response:**
```typescript
{
  updated_tasks: Array<{
    task_id: string;
    status: string;          // always "done"
    updated: boolean;        // success flag
  }>;
}
```
Returns HTTP 200.

**Key details:**
- Requires `graph:reason` scope
- **YES, commit check functionality EXISTS and WORKS:**
  - Extracts task references from commit message using regex (`extractReferencedTaskIds()`)
  - Filters to only tasks that exist in the workspace
  - Updates matched tasks to status `"done"` via `processCommitTaskRefs()`
  - Returns array of update results with success flags
- Calls `processCommitTaskRefs()` from `/app/src/server/mcp/commit-check.ts` (which wraps `extractReferencedTaskIds()` from `../webhook/commit-task-refs`)

---

### 4. **Orchestrator Accept/Abort/Reject Handlers** (`app/src/server/orchestrator/routes.ts`)

**Accept** (`POST /api/orchestrator/:workspaceId/sessions/:sessionId/accept`)
- **Request body:** `{ summary?: string }`
- **Response:** `{ accepted: true, taskStatus: "done" }` (HTTP 200)

**Abort** (`POST /api/orchestrator/:workspaceId/sessions/:sessionId/abort`)
- **Request body:** (none)
- **Response:** `{ aborted: true, taskStatus: "ready" }` (HTTP 200)

**Reject** (`POST /api/orchestrator/:workspaceId/sessions/:sessionId/reject`)
- **Request body:** `{ feedback: string }` (required, non-empty)
- **Response:** `{ rejected: true, continuing: true }` (HTTP 200)

**Status** (`GET /api/orchestrator/:workspaceId/sessions/:sessionId`)
- **Response:** 
```typescript
{
  agentSessionId: string;
  orchestratorStatus: string;
  worktreeBranch?: string;
  worktreePath?: string;
  startedAt?: string;
  lastEventAt?: string;
  error?: string;
}
```

All orchestrator handlers require workspace membership (validated via `member_of` table).

---

### File Locations:
- **MCP handlers:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/app/src/server/mcp/mcp-route.ts`
- **Commit check core:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/app/src/server/mcp/commit-check.ts`
- **MCP queries:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/app/src/server/mcp/mcp-queries.ts`
- **Orchestrator routes:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/app/src/server/orchestrator/routes.ts`
- **Orchestrator lifecycle:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/app/src/server/orchestrator/session-lifecycle.ts`

```
## Summary: MCP Authentication Requirements

The MCP endpoints at `/api/mcp/:workspaceId/*` require **OAuth 2.1 JWT Bearer token authentication**. The "401 missing or invalid Authorization header" error occurs when requests lack a valid JWT token.

### Key Authentication Files

1. **`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/app/src/server/mcp/auth.ts`**
   - Main authentication middleware: `authenticateMcpRequest()`
   - Validates `Authorization: Bearer <token>` header format
   - Verifies JWT signature against JWKS (JSON Web Key Set)
   - Resolves person → identity → workspace membership
   - Checks token claims for workspace/agent type

2. **`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/app/src/server/mcp/token-validation.ts`**
   - JWT validator factory: `createJwtValidator(issuerUrl)`
   - Uses `verifyAccessToken()` from better-auth/oauth2
   - Validates JWKS, issuer, and audience claims

### Token Validation Requirements

Tokens must contain these claims:
- **sub**: person/user ID (required)
- **scope**: space-separated permissions (e.g., "graph:read task:write")
- **urn:brain:workspace**: optional workspace ID (must match requested workspace if present)
- **urn:brain:agent_type**: optional agent type (defaults to "code_agent")

### Valid Agent Types
`code_agent`, `architect`, `management`, `design_partner`, `observer`

### How to Obtain OAuth Tokens (from smoke tests)

The working implementation is shown in:
- `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/tests/smoke/oauth-mcp-auth.test.ts` (complete flow)
- `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/tests/smoke/intent-context.test.ts` (getOAuthToken helper)

**Token acquisition flow:**
1. **Sign up**: `POST /api/auth/sign-up/email` → get session token
2. **Register OAuth client**: `POST /api/auth/oauth2/register` (public client, no auth method) → get client_id
3. **Get authorization code**: `GET /api/auth/oauth2/authorize` with PKCE + `resource=${baseUrl}` parameter → 302 redirect with code
4. **Exchange for JWT**: `POST /api/auth/oauth2/token` with authorization code + code_verifier + `resource=${baseUrl}` → JWT access_token
5. **Use token**: Send `Authorization: Bearer ${access_token}` on MCP requests

**Critical detail**: The `resource` parameter in both authorize and token endpoints **must be set** to trigger JWT (not opaque token) issuance.

### Error Responses

- **401**: Missing/invalid header, empty token, or invalid/expired JWT
- **403**: Token workspace mismatch, identity not found, not a workspace member, or insufficient scope
- **404**: Workspace not found
- **400**: Invalid agent type

### Scope Enforcement

Routes enforce specific scopes:
- `graph:read` for read-only endpoints
- `task:write`, `decision:write`, `observation:write`, `question:write` for write endpoints
- Insufficient scope returns 403

    65→async function getOAuthToken(
    66→  baseUrl: string,
    67→  surreal: Surreal,
    68→  sessionHeaders: Record<string, string>,
    69→): Promise<string> {
    70→  const dcrRes = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/oauth2/register`, {
    71→    method: "POST",
    72→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    73→    body: JSON.stringify({
    74→      client_name: "intent-test-client",
    75→      redirect_uris: ["http://127.0.0.1:9999/callback"],
    76→      grant_types: ["authorization_code", "refresh_token"],
    77→      response_types: ["code"],
    78→      token_endpoint_auth_method: "none",
    79→    }),
    80→  });
    81→  if (!dcrRes.ok) throw new Error(`DCR failed: ${dcrRes.status} ${await dcrRes.text()}`);
    82→
    83→  const { client_id } = (await dcrRes.json()) as { client_id: string };
    84→
    85→  await surreal.query(`UPDATE oauthClient SET skipConsent = true WHERE clientId = $cid;`, {
    86→    cid: client_id,
    87→  });
    88→
    89→  const { verifier, challenge } = generatePkce();
    90→  const authUrl = new URL(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/oauth2/authorize`);
    91→  authUrl.searchParams.set("client_id", client_id);
    92→  authUrl.searchParams.set("redirect_uri", "http://127.0.0.1:9999/callback");
    93→  authUrl.searchParams.set("response_type", "code");
    94→  authUrl.searchParams.set("scope", "graph:read graph:reason offline_access");
    95→  authUrl.searchParams.set("state", "test-state");
    96→  authUrl.searchParams.set("code_challenge", challenge);
    97→  authUrl.searchParams.set("code_challenge_method", "S256");
    98→  authUrl.searchParams.set("resource", baseUrl);
    99→
   100→  const authRes = await fetch(authUrl.toString(), {
   101→    headers: sessionHeaders,
   102→    redirect: "manual",
   103→  });
   104→  if (authRes.status !== 302) throw new Error(`Authorize did not redirect: ${authRes.status}`);
   105→
   106→  const location = authRes.headers.get("location")!;
   107→  const redirectUrl = new URL(location, baseUrl);
   108→  const code = redirectUrl.searchParams.get("code") ?? "";
   109→  if (!code) throw new Error(`No code in redirect: ${location}`);
   110→
   111→  const tokenRes = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/oauth2/token`, {
   112→    method: "POST",
   113→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
   114→    body: new URLSearchParams({
   115→      grant_type: "authorization_code",
   116→      code,
   117→      redirect_uri: "http://127.0.0.1:9999/callback",
   118→      client_id,
   119→      code_verifier: verifier,
   120→      resource: baseUrl,
   121→    }),
   122→  });
   123→  if (!tokenRes.ok) throw new Error(`Token exchange failed: ${tokenRes.status} ${await tokenRes.text()}`);
   124→
   125→  const tokens = (await tokenRes.json()) as { access_token: string };
   126→  return tokens.access_token;
   127→}

   130→  workspaceId: string;
   131→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
   132→  accessToken: string;
   133→};
   134→
   135→async function createWorkspaceWithOAuth(
   136→  baseUrl: string,

   138→  name?: string,
   139→): Promise<AuthedWorkspace> {

   141→  const { userId, headers: sessionHeaders } = await signUpAndGetSession(baseUrl, email, "Intent Tester");
   142→
   143→  const workspaceId = randomUUID();
   144→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);

    23→export function setupSmokeSuite(suiteName: string): () => SmokeTestRuntime {

    43→      10_000,
    44→      "authenticate with SurrealDB",
    45→    );
    46→
    47→    await withTimeout(() => surreal.query(`DEFINE NAMESPACE ${namespace};`), 10_000, "define test namespace");
    48→    await withTimeout(() => surreal.use({ namespace }), 10_000, "switch to test namespace");
    49→    await withTimeout(() => surreal.query(`DEFINE DATABASE ${database};`), 10_000, "define test database");
    50→    await withTimeout(
    51→      () => surreal.use({ namespace, database }),
    52→      10_000,
    53→      "switch to test namespace/database",
    54→    );
    55→
    56→    const schemaSql = readFileSync(join(process.cwd(), "schema", "surreal-schema.surql"), "utf8");
    57→    await withTimeout(() => surreal.query(schemaSql), 20_000, "apply schema");
    58→
    59→    const config: ServerConfig = {
    60→      openRouterApiKey: requireTestEnv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY"),
    61→      chatAgentModelId: requireTestEnv("CHAT_AGENT_MODEL"),
    62→      extractionModelId: requireTestEnv("EXTRACTION_MODEL"),

    69→      surrealUrl,

    72→      surrealNamespace: namespace,
    73→      surrealDatabase: database,
    74→      port: 0, // OS-assigned
    75→      betterAuthSecret: process.env.BETTER_AUTH_SECRET ?? "smoke-test-secret-at-least-32-chars-long",
    76→      betterAuthUrl: "http://127.0.0.1:0", // placeholder, updated after server starts
    77→      githubClientId: process.env.GITHUB_CLIENT_ID ?? "smoke-test-github-id",
    78→      githubClientSecret: process.env.GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET ?? "smoke-test-github-secret",
    79→    };
    80→
    81→    const deps = await createRuntimeDependencies(config);
    82→    runtimeSurreal = deps.surreal;
    83→    analyticsSurreal = deps.analyticsSurreal;
    84→
    85→    const serverDeps: ServerDependencies = {
    86→      config,
    87→      surreal: deps.surreal,
    88→      analyticsSurreal: deps.analyticsSurreal,
    89→      auth: deps.auth,
    90→      chatAgentModel: deps.chatAgentModel,
    91→      extractionModel: deps.extractionModel,
    92→      pmAgentModel: deps.pmAgentModel,
    93→      analyticsAgentModel: deps.analyticsAgentModel,
    94→      embeddingModel: deps.embeddingModel,
    95→      sse: createSseRegistry(),
    96→    };
    97→
    98→    server = createBrainServer(serverDeps);
    99→    const port = server.port;
   100→    const baseUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`;
   101→    // Update betterAuthUrl now that we know the actual port
   102→    config.betterAuthUrl = baseUrl;
   103→
   104→    runtime = { baseUrl, surreal, namespace, database, port };
   105→    setupSucceeded = true;
   106→  }, 60_000);
   107→
   108→  afterAll(async () => {
   109→    if (server) {
   110→      server.stop(true);
   111→    }
   112→
   113→    if (runtimeSurreal) {
   114→      await runtimeSurreal.close().catch(() => undefined);
   115→    }
   116→    if (analyticsSurreal) {

   118→    }
   119→
   120→    if (!runtime) {

   123→
   124→    if (setupSucceeded && !process.env.SMOKE_KEEP_DB) {
   125→      try {
   126→        await withTimeout(() => runtime!.surreal.query(`REMOVE DATABASE ${database};`), 10_000, "remove test database");
   127→      } catch {
   128→        // Best effort cleanup.
   129→      }
   130→
   131→      try {
   132→        await withTimeout(() => runtime!.surreal.query(`REMOVE NAMESPACE ${namespace};`), 10_000, "remove test namespace");
   133→      } catch {
   134→        // Best effort cleanup.
   135→      }
   136→    }
   137→
   138→    await withTimeout(() => runtime!.surreal.close(), 2_000, "close SurrealDB").catch(() => undefined);
   139→  }, 20_000);
   140→
   141→  return () => {
   142→    if (!runtime) {
   143→      throw new Error("Smoke runtime requested before suite setup completed");
   144→    }
   145→    return runtime;
   146→  };
   147→}

   149→export type TestUser = {
   150→  headers: Record<string, string>;
   151→};
   152→
   153→export async function createTestUser(baseUrl: string, suffix: string): Promise<TestUser> {
   154→  const email = `smoke-${Date.now()}-${suffix}@test.local`;
   155→  const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/sign-up/email`, {
   156→    method: "POST",
   157→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   158→    body: JSON.stringify({ name: "Smoke Tester", email, password=[REDACTED]" }),
   159→    redirect: "manual",
   160→  });
   161→
   162→  if (!response.ok) {
   163→    const body = await response.text();
   164→    throw new Error(`Failed to create test user (${response.status}): ${body}`);
   165→  }
   166→
   167→  const setCookie = response.headers.getSetCookie();
   168→  if (!setCookie || setCookie.length === 0) {
   169→    throw new Error("Sign-up did not return session cookies");
   170→  }
   171→
   172→  const cookieHeader = setCookie.map((c) => c.split(";")[0]).join("; ");
   173→  return { headers: { Cookie: cookieHeader } };
   174→}
   175→
   176→export async function collectSseEvents<T extends { type: string }>(streamUrl: string, timeoutMs: number): Promise<T[]> {
   177→  const response = await fetch(streamUrl, { headers: { Accept: "text/event-stream" } });
   178→  if (!response.ok || !response.body) {
   179→    throw new Error(`Failed to open SSE stream (${response.status})`);
   180→  }
   181→
   182→  const reader = response.body.getReader();
   183→  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
   184→  const events: T[] = [];
   185→  let buffer = "";
   186→
   187→  const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
   188→    void reader.cancel();
   189→  }, timeoutMs);
   190→
   191→  try {
   192→    while (true) {
   193→      const { value, done } = await reader.read();
   194→      if (done) {
   195→        break;
   196→      }
   197→
   198→      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
   199→      const segments = buffer.split("\n\n");
   200→      buffer = segments.pop() ?? "";
   201→
   202→      for (const segment of segments) {
   203→        const dataLine = segment.split("\n").find((line) => line.startsWith("data: "));
   204→        if (!dataLine) {
   205→          continue;
   206→        }
   207→
   208→        const event = JSON.parse(dataLine.slice("data: ".length)) as T;
   209→        events.push(event);
   210→
   211→        if (event.type === "error") {
   212→          const errorText = (event as { error?: string }).error;
   213→          throw new Error(`SSE error event: ${errorText ?? "unknown error"}`);
   214→        }
   215→
   216→        if (event.type === "done") {
   217→          return events;
   218→        }
   219→      }
   220→    }
   221→  } finally {
   222→    clearTimeout(timeout);
   223→    reader.releaseLock();
   224→  }
   225→
   226→  return events;
   227→}
   228→
   229→export async function fetchJson<T>(url: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
   230→  const response = await fetch(url, init);
   231→  if (!response.ok) {
   232→    const body = await response.text();
   233→    throw new Error(`Request failed (${response.status}) ${url}: ${body}`);
   234→  }
   235→
   236→  return (await response.json()) as T;
   237→}
   238→
   239→function requireTestEnv(name: string): string {
   240→  const value = process.env[name];
   241→  if (!value || value.trim().length === 0) {
   242→    throw new Error(`Smoke test requires env var ${name}`);
   243→  }
   244→  return value;
   245→}
   246→
   247→async function withTimeout<T>(callback: () => Promise<T>, timeoutMs: number, label: string): Promise<T> {
   248→  return await Promise.race([
   249→    callback(),
   250→    new Promise<T>((_, reject) => {
   251→      setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`Timed out: ${label}`)), timeoutMs);
   252→    }),
   253→  ]);
   254→}
   255→

  "new_string": "function base64url(buf: Buffer): string {\n  return buf.toString(\"base64url\");\n}\n\nfunction generatePkce(): { verifier: string; challenge: string } {\n  const verifier = base64url(randomBytes(32));\n  const challenge = base64url(createHash(\"sha256\").update(verifier).digest());\n  return { verifier, challenge };\n}\n\n/**\n * Obtain an OAuth2 JWT access token for MCP endpoints.\n * Requires a SurrealDB connection to skip consent screen.\n */\nexport async function getOAuthToken(\n  baseUrl: string,\n  surreal: Surreal,\n  sessionHeaders: Record<string, string>,\n  scopes: string = \"graph:read graph:reason session:write offline_access\",\n): Promise<string> {\n  const dcrRes = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/oauth2/register`, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n    body: JSON.stringify({\n      client_name: `smoke-test-${Date.now()}`,\n      redirect_uris: [\"http://127.0.0.1:9999/callback\"],\n      grant_types: [\"authorization_code\", \"refresh_token\"],\n      response_types: [\"code\"],\n      token_endpoint_auth_method: \"none\",\n    }),\n  });\n  if (!dcrRes.ok) throw new Error(`DCR failed: ${dcrRes.status} ${await dcrRes.text()}`);\n\n  const { client_id } = (await dcrRes.json()) as { client_id: string };\n\n  await surreal.query(`UPDATE oauthClient SET skipConsent = true WHERE clientId = $cid;`, {\n    cid: client_id,\n  });\n\n  const { verifier, challenge } = generatePkce();\n  const authUrl = new URL(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/oauth2/authorize`);\n  authUrl.searchParams.set(\"client_id\", client_id);\n  authUrl.searchParams.set(\"redirect_uri\", \"http://127.0.0.1:9999/callback\");\n  authUrl.searchParams.set(\"response_type\", \"code\");\n  authUrl.searchParams.set(\"scope\", scopes);\n  authUrl.searchParams.set(\"state\", \"test-state\");\n  authUrl.searchParams.set(\"code_challenge\", challenge);\n  authUrl.searchParams.set(\"code_challenge_method\", \"S256\");\n  authUrl.searchParams.set(\"resource\", baseUrl);\n\n  const authRes = await fetch(authUrl.toString(), {\n    headers: sessionHeaders,\n    redirect: \"manual\",\n  });\n  if (authRes.status !== 302) throw new Error(`Authorize did not redirect: ${authRes.status}`);\n\n  const location = authRes.headers.get(\"location\")!;\n  const redirectUrl = new URL(location, baseUrl);\n  const code = redirectUrl.searchParams.get(\"code\") ?? \"\";\n  if (!code) throw new Error(`No code in redirect: ${location}`);\n\n  const tokenRes = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/oauth2/token`, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/x-www-form-urlencoded\" },\n    body: new URLSearchParams({\n      grant_type: \"authorization_code\",\n      code,\n      redirect_uri: \"http://127.0.0.1:9999/callback\",\n      client_id,\n      code_verifier: verifier,\n      resource: baseUrl,\n    }),\n  });\n  if (!tokenRes.ok) throw new Error(`Token exchange failed: ${tokenRes.status} ${await tokenRes.text()}`);\n\n  const tokens = (await tokenRes.json()) as { access_token: string };\n  return tokens.access_token;\n}\n\nexport type TestUserWithMcp = TestUser & {\n  mcpHeaders: Record<string, string>;\n};\n\n/**\n * Create a test user with both session cookie and OAuth MCP token.\n */\nexport async function createTestUserWithMcp(\n  baseUrl: string,\n  surreal: Surreal,\n  suffix: string,\n  scopes?: string,\n): Promise<TestUserWithMcp> {\n  const user = await createTestUser(baseUrl, suffix);\n  const accessToken = await getOAuthToken(baseUrl, surreal, user.headers, scopes);\n  return {\n    ...user,\n    mcpHeaders: { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction requireTestEnv(name: string): string {"
}

    19→  tasks: Array<{ id: string; title: string; status: string }>,
    20→) {
    21→  const user = await createTestUser(baseUrl, `commit-check-${suffix}`);
    22→
    23→  const workspace = await fetchJson<{ workspaceId: string }>(
    24→    `${baseUrl}/api/workspaces`,
    25→    {
    26→      method: "POST",
    27→      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...user.headers },
    28→      body: JSON.stringify({ name: `Commit Check ${Date.now()} ${suffix}` }),
    29→    },
    30→  );
    31→
    32→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspace.workspaceId);
    33→  const taskRecords: Array<{ id: string; record: RecordId<"task", string> }> = [];
    34→
    35→  for (const task of tasks) {
    36→    const taskRecord = new RecordId("task", task.id);
    37→    await surreal.create(taskRecord).content({
    38→      workspace: workspaceRecord,
    39→      title: task.title,
    40→      status: task.status,
    41→      category: "engineering",
    42→      priority: "medium",
    43→      created_at: new Date(),
    44→      updated_at: new Date(),
    45→    });
    46→    taskRecords.push({ id: task.id, record: taskRecord });
    47→  }
    48→
    49→  return { user, workspace, workspaceRecord, taskRecords };
    50→}
    51→
    52→describe("commit-check endpoint sets tasks to done (US-3)", () => {
    53→  // Walking skeleton: commit with task ref sets task to done
    54→
    55→  it("Given a task in_progress and a commit referencing it, When commit-check runs, Then the task status becomes 'done'", async () => {
    56→    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();
    57→    const taskId = `cc-task-${Date.now()}-single`;
    58→
    59→    const { user, workspace, taskRecords } = await setupWorkspaceWithTasks(
    60→      baseUrl, surreal, "single-1",
    61→      [{ id: taskId, title: "Implement login flow", status: "in_progress" }],
    62→    );
    63→
    64→    const res = await fetch(
    65→      `${baseUrl}/api/mcp/${workspace.workspaceId}/commits/post-check`,
    66→      {
    67→        method: "POST",
    68→        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...user.headers },
    69→        body: JSON.stringify({
    70→          message: `Implement login flow\n\ntask:${taskId}`,
    71→        }),
    72→      },
    73→    );
    74→
    75→    expect(res.ok).toBe(true);
    76→
    77→    // Verify task status changed to done
    78→    const [taskRows] = await surreal
    79→      .query<[Array<{ status: string }>]>(
    80→        "SELECT status FROM $task;",
    81→        { task: taskRecords[0]!.record },
    82→      )
    83→      .collect<[Array<{ status: string }>]>();
    84→
    85→    expect(taskRows[0]?.status).toBe("done");
    86→  }, 30_000);
    87→
    88→  it("Given multiple tasks in_progress, When commit-check runs with message referencing both, Then both tasks become 'done'", async () => {
    89→    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();
    90→    const taskId1 = `cc-task-${Date.now()}-multi-a`;
    91→    const taskId2 = `cc-task-${Date.now()}-multi-b`;
    92→
    93→    const { user, workspace, taskRecords } = await setupWorkspaceWithTasks(
    94→      baseUrl, surreal, "multi-1",
    95→      [
    96→        { id: taskId1, title: "Auth flow", status: "in_progress" },
    97→        { id: taskId2, title: "Token refresh", status: "in_progress" },
    98→      ],
    99→    );
   100→
   101→    await fetch(
   102→      `${baseUrl}/api/mcp/${workspace.workspaceId}/commits/post-check`,
   103→      {
   104→        method: "POST",
   105→        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...user.headers },
   106→        body: JSON.stringify({
   107→          message: `Batch update\n\ntasks: ${taskId1}, ${taskId2}`,
   108→        }),
   109→      },
   110→    );
   111→
   112→    for (const tr of taskRecords) {
   113→      const [rows] = await surreal
   114→        .query<[Array<{ status: string }>]>(
   115→          "SELECT status FROM $task;",
   116→          { task: tr.record },
   117→        )
   118→        .collect<[Array<{ status: string }>]>();
   119→      expect(rows[0]?.status).toBe("done");
   120→    }
   121→  }, 30_000);

   139→        body: JSON.stringify({ message: "Fix typo in README" }),
   140→      },
   141→    );
   142→
   143→    expect(res.ok).toBe(true);
   144→
   145→    const [taskRows] = await surreal
   146→      .query<[Array<{ status: string }>]>(
   147→        "SELECT status FROM $task;",
   148→        { task: taskRecords[0]!.record },
   149→      )
   150→      .collect<[Array<{ status: string }>]>();
   151→
   152→    expect(taskRows[0]?.status).toBe("in_progress");
   153→  }, 30_000);
   154→
   155→  it("Given a task already 'done', When commit-check runs referencing it, Then the task remains 'done' and no error occurs (idempotent)", async () => {
   156→    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();
   157→    const taskId = `cc-task-${Date.now()}-idempotent`;

The fix: reserve a port first, then use it for both `betterAuthUrl` and the server:

> AGENT

I see the problem! `validateToken` is a module-level `let` variable (line 16). It's lazily initialized on first use. Since test suites run in the same process, the first suite's `createJwtValidator(issuerUrl)` captures the first server's URL. When subsequent suites call MCP endpoints, the cached validator still points to the first server (which is already shut down → "Unable to connect").

This is a cross-suite contamination issue. The fix is that each suite needs its own server, and the JWKS validator uses `issuerUrl` from the current request context, not a stale cached one.

But I shouldn't change production code for this. Instead, I can make the tests run sequentially with `--concurrency 1`, or I can fix the module-level cache. Actually, the better fix is to make the tests each run independently - let me check if they share a process.

Actually wait — the module-level `let validateToken` is the real issue even for a single suite. If the first suite uses port X, `validateToken` caches that. When the second suite starts on port Y, the cached validator still points to port X (now dead). Let me fix this in the production code — the validator should not be module-level cached since `issuerUrl` can change:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit

  "old_string": "let validateToken: ((token: string) => Promise<BrainTokenClaims>) | undefined;",
  "new_string": "let validateToken: ((token: string) => Promise<BrainTokenClaims>) | undefined;\nlet cachedIssuerUrl: string | undefined;"
}

> AGENT

Good progress — 17 pass, 5 fail. The remaining failures are:
1. **Backward transitions (3)**: "missing orchestrator_status" — sessions created via MCP don't have `orchestrator_status` field
2. **Session accept (2)**: Same "missing orchestrator_status" issue

The tests create sessions via the MCP `/sessions/start` endpoint, then try to orchestrator accept/abort/reject them. But the orchestrator expects `orchestrator_status` on the session. The MCP session start doesn't set this field because it's for agent sessions (not orchestrator-managed ones). 

The tests need to create sessions via the orchestrator's assignment flow instead, or the sessions need `orchestrator_status`. Let me check what the orchestrator expects:

give me ur reason for why u think the test assertion is incorrect

> AGENT

The original test expected `"ready"` after reject, but the server implementation in `session-lifecycle.ts` explicitly sets the task to `"in_progress"` on reject:

```typescript
// rejectOrchestratorSession (lines ~693-714)
// 1. Update session status to active (agent resumes work)
await input.surreal.update(sessionRecord).merge({
  orchestrator_status: "active" as OrchestratorStatus,
  last_feedback: input.feedback,
});
// 2. Return task to in_progress
if (session.task_id) {
  await input.surreal.update(session.task_id).merge({
    status: "in_progress",
    updated_at: new Date(),
  });
}
```

The semantic distinction:

- **Reject** = "work not acceptable, try again" → session goes back to `active`, task stays `in_progress` (agent resumes)
- **Abort** = "abandon this session entirely" → task resets to `ready` (available for a fresh attempt)

The test title already matched this intent: *"When the session is rejected, Then the task status remains 'in_progress' (agent resumes work)"* — I updated both the title and the assertion to align with what the server actually does.

So the original assertion (`"ready"`) was wrong relative to the implementation. The test was written with abort semantics but labeled as reject.

Now let me verify the rest of the smoke tests still pass alongside our changes.

     1→import { describe, expect, it } from "bun:test";
     2→import { createHash, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
     3→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     4→import { setupSmokeSuite } from "./smoke-test-kit";
     5→
     6→function base64url(buf: Buffer): string {
     7→  return buf.toString("base64url");
     8→}
     9→
    10→function generatePkce(): { verifier: string; challenge: string } {
    11→  const verifier = base64url(randomBytes(32));
    12→  const challenge = base64url(createHash("sha256").update(verifier).digest());
    13→  return { verifier, challenge };
    14→}
    15→
    16→const getRuntime = setupSmokeSuite("oauth-mcp-auth");
    17→
    18→/** Sign up a test user and get a session token via better-auth API */
    19→async function signUpAndGetSession(baseUrl: string, email: string, name: string): Promise<{
    20→  userId: string;
    21→  sessionToken: string;
    22→  headers: Record<string, string>;
    23→}> {
    24→  const res = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/sign-up/email`, {
    25→    method: "POST",
    26→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    27→    body: JSON.stringify({ email, password=[REDACTED]!", name }),
    28→  });
    29→
    30→  if (!res.ok) {
    31→    throw new Error(`Sign up failed: ${res.status} ${await res.text()}`);
    32→  }
    33→
    34→  // Sign-up returns user + session in JSON body
    35→  const data = await res.json() as { user: { id: string }; token: string };
    36→
    37→  // Also extract session cookie for subsequent requests
    38→  const cookies = res.headers.getSetCookie();
    39→  const sessionCookie = cookies.find(c => c.startsWith("better-auth.session_token="));
    40→  const sessionToken = sessionCookie
    41→    ? decodeURIComponent(sessionCookie.split("=")[1].split(";")[0])
    42→    : data.token;
    43→
    44→  return {
    45→    userId: data.user.id,
    46→    sessionToken,
    47→    headers: { Cookie: `better-auth.session_token=${sessionToken}` },
    48→  };
    49→}
    50→
    51→/** Register an OAuth client via DCR and get tokens via the authorization flow */
    52→async function getOAuthTokens(
    53→  baseUrl: string,
    54→  surreal: import("surrealdb").Surreal,
    55→  sessionHeaders: Record<string, string>,
    56→  scopes: string,
    57→): Promise<{
    58→  clientId: string;
    59→  accessToken: string;
    60→  refreshToken: string;
    61→  expiresIn: number;
    62→}> {
    63→  // 1. Register a public client
    64→  const dcrRes = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/oauth2/register`, {
    65→    method: "POST",
    66→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    67→    body: JSON.stringify({
    68→      client_name: "test-client",
    69→      redirect_uris: ["http://127.0.0.1:9999/callback"],
    70→      grant_types: ["authorization_code", "refresh_token"],
    71→      response_types: ["code"],
    72→      token_endpoint_auth_method: "none",
    73→    }),
    74→  });
    75→
    76→  if (!dcrRes.ok) {
    77→    throw new Error(`DCR failed: ${dcrRes.status} ${await dcrRes.text()}`);
    78→  }
    79→
    80→  const { client_id } = await dcrRes.json() as { client_id: string };
    81→
    82→  // Skip consent for test clients (authorize endpoint checks skip_consent on the client record)
    83→  await surreal.query(
    84→    `UPDATE oauthClient SET skipConsent = true WHERE clientId = $cid;`,
    85→    { cid: client_id },
    86→  );
    87→
    88→  // 2. Get authorization code with PKCE (required for public clients)
    89→  const { verifier, challenge } = generatePkce();
    90→  const authUrl = new URL(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/oauth2/authorize`);
    91→  authUrl.searchParams.set("client_id", client_id);
    92→  authUrl.searchParams.set("redirect_uri", "http://127.0.0.1:9999/callback");
    93→  authUrl.searchParams.set("response_type", "code");
    94→  authUrl.searchParams.set("scope", scopes);
    95→  authUrl.searchParams.set("state", "test-state");
    96→  authUrl.searchParams.set("code_challenge", challenge);
    97→  authUrl.searchParams.set("code_challenge_method", "S256");
    98→  // Pass resource so better-auth issues a JWT access token (not opaque)
    99→  authUrl.searchParams.set("resource", baseUrl);
   100→
   101→  const authRes = await fetch(authUrl.toString(), {
   102→    headers: sessionHeaders,
   103→    redirect: "manual",
   104→  });
   105→
   106→  if (authRes.status !== 302) {
   107→    const body = await authRes.text();
   108→    throw new Error(`Authorize did not redirect: ${authRes.status} body=${body} headers=${JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(authRes.headers.entries()))}`);
   109→  }
   110→
   111→  const location = authRes.headers.get("location");
   112→  if (!location) throw new Error("No location header in auth redirect");
   113→  const redirectUrl = new URL(location, baseUrl);
   114→  const code = redirectUrl.searchParams.get("code") ?? "";
   115→  const error = redirectUrl.searchParams.get("error");
   116→  if (!code) throw new Error(`No code in redirect: ${location} (error=${error})`);
   117→
   118→  // 3. Exchange code for tokens
   119→  const tokenRes = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/oauth2/token`, {
   120→    method: "POST",
   121→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
   122→    body: new URLSearchParams({
   123→      grant_type: "authorization_code",
   124→      code,
   125→      redirect_uri: "http://127.0.0.1:9999/callback",
   126→      client_id,
   127→      code_verifier: verifier,
   128→      resource: baseUrl,
   129→    }),
   130→  });
   131→
   132→  if (!tokenRes.ok) {
   133→    throw new Error(`Token exchange failed: ${tokenRes.status} ${await tokenRes.text()}`);
   134→  }
   135→
   136→  const tokens = await tokenRes.json() as {
   137→    access_token: string;
   138→    refresh_token: string;
   139→    expires_in: number;
   140→    token_type: string;
   141→  };
   142→
   143→  return {
   144→    clientId: client_id,
   145→    accessToken: tokens.access_token,
   146→    refreshToken: tokens.refresh_token,
   147→    expiresIn: tokens.expires_in,
   148→  };
   149→}
   150→
   151→/** Helper: POST to an MCP endpoint with Bearer token */
   152→function mcpPost(baseUrl: string, workspaceId: string, path: string, token: string, body: unknown = {}) {
   153→  return fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/mcp/${workspaceId}${path}`, {
   154→    method: "POST",
   155→    headers: {
   156→      "Content-Type": "application/json",
   157→      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
   158→    },
   159→    body: JSON.stringify(body),
   160→  });
   161→}
   162→
   163→/** Helper: GET an MCP endpoint with Bearer token */
   164→function mcpGet(baseUrl: string, workspaceId: string, path: string, token: string) {
   165→  return fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/mcp/${workspaceId}${path}`, {
   166→    headers: {
   167→      "Content-Type": "application/json",
   168→      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
   169→    },
   170→  });
   171→}
   172→
   173→describe("OAuth MCP Auth", () => {
   174→  const ALL_SCOPES = "graph:read graph:reason decision:write task:write observation:write question:write session:write offline_access";
   175→  let workspaceId: string;

   184→    await surreal.query(

   186→        name: "OAuth Test Workspace",
   187→        status: "active",
   188→        onboarding_complete: true,

   190→        onboarding_summary_pending: false,

   193→      };`,
   194→      { ws: wsRecord },
   195→    );
   196→
   197→    // Create project linked to workspace
   198→    const projRecord = new RecordId("project", crypto.randomUUID());
   199→    await surreal.query(
   200→      `CREATE $proj CONTENT {
   201→        name: "Test Project",
   202→        status: "active",
   203→        workspace: $ws,
   204→        created_at: time::now()
   205→      };`,
   206→      { proj: projRecord, ws: wsRecord },
   207→    );
   208→    await surreal.query(
   209→      `RELATE $ws->has_project->$proj SET added_at = time::now();`,

   212→
   213→    // Sign up a user
   214→    const { userId } = await signUpAndGetSession(baseUrl, "oauth-test@example.com", "OAuth Tester");
   215→
   216→    // Create identity + spoke edge, then member_of (identity -> workspace)
   217→    const personRecord = new RecordId("person", userId);
   218→    const identityRecord = new RecordId("identity", crypto.randomUUID());

   221→      { identity: identityRecord, ws: wsRecord },
   222→    );
   223→    await surreal.query(
   224→      `RELATE $identity->identity_person->$person SET added_at = time::now();`,

   227→    await surreal.query(

   229→      { identity: identityRecord, ws: wsRecord },

   231→
   232→    // Trigger JWKS key generation by hitting the JWKS endpoint

   235→    // Get OAuth tokens with all scopes

   237→    const tokens = await getOAuthTokens(baseUrl, surreal, session.headers, ALL_SCOPES);
   238→    fullToken = tokens.accessToken;
   239→
   240→    expect(fullToken).toBeTruthy();
   241→  }, 30_000);
   242→
   243→  it("MCP route with valid JWT returns data", async () => {
   244→    const { baseUrl } = getRuntime();

   246→    expect(res.status).toBe(200);

     1→import { describe, expect, it } from "bun:test";
     2→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { createTestUser, fetchJson, setupSmokeSuite } from "../smoke-test-kit";
     4→
     5→/**
     6→ * US-UI-004: Auth Rewiring -- Identity resolution from person via spoke
     7→ *
     8→ * Validates that the auth layer resolves identity from person via spoke traversal:
     9→ * - better-auth still uses person as user model (session.person_id, account.person_id)
    10→ * - Identity is resolved from person via identity_person spoke edge
    11→ * - Chat ingress resolves identity and passes it through the pipeline
    12→ * - MCP auth resolves identity from JWT sub (person ID) via spoke
    13→ */
    14→
    15→const getRuntime = setupSmokeSuite("auth-rewiring");
    16→
    17→describe("US-UI-004: Auth resolves identity from person via spoke traversal", () => {
    18→  // -- Session still references person --
    19→
    20→  it("Given a user signs up, when the session is queried, then session.person_id references a person record", async () => {
    21→    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();
    22→
    23→    await createTestUser(baseUrl, "auth-identity");
    24→
    25→    const [sessions] = await surreal.query<
    26→      [Array<{ person_id: RecordId }>]
    27→    >("SELECT person_id FROM session LIMIT 1;");
    28→
    29→    expect(sessions.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    30→    expect(sessions[0].person_id).toBeDefined();
    31→    expect((sessions[0].person_id as RecordId).table.name).toBe("person");
    32→  }, 60_000);
    33→
    34→  // -- Person has identity via spoke edge --
    35→
    36→  it("Given a user signs up and creates a workspace, when the identity_person spoke is queried, then the person has a linked identity with type human", async () => {
    37→    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();
    38→
    39→    const user = await createTestUser(baseUrl, "auth-spoke");
    40→
    41→    // Create workspace to trigger identity bootstrap
    42→    const workspace = await fetchJson<{ workspaceId: string }>(
    43→      `${baseUrl}/api/workspaces`,
    44→      {
    45→        method: "POST",
    46→        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...user.headers },
    47→        body: JSON.stringify({ name: `Spoke Test ${Date.now()}` }),
    48→      },
    49→    );
    50→
    51→    const wsRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspace.workspaceId);
    52→
    53→    // Find identity created by bootstrap for this workspace
    54→    const [identities] = await surreal.query<
    55→      [Array<{ id: RecordId; type: string; name: string }>]
    56→    >(
    57→      "SELECT id, type, name FROM identity WHERE workspace = $ws AND type = 'human' LIMIT 1;",
    58→      { ws: wsRecord },
    59→    );
    60→
    61→    expect(identities.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    62→    expect(identities[0].type).toBe("human");
    63→
    64→    // Verify spoke edge exists from this identity to a person
    65→    const [spokeEdges] = await surreal.query<[Array<{ out: RecordId }>]>(
    66→      "SELECT out FROM identity_person WHERE in = $identity LIMIT 1;",
    67→      { identity: identities[0].id },
    68→    );
    69→    expect(spokeEdges.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    70→    expect((spokeEdges[0].out as RecordId).table.name).toBe("person");
    71→  }, 60_000);
    72→
    73→  // -- Identity is member_of workspace --
    74→
    75→  it("Given a user creates a workspace, when member_of is queried, then the identity (not person) is the member", async () => {
    76→    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();
    77→
    78→    const user = await createTestUser(baseUrl, "auth-member");
    79→    const workspace = await fetchJson<{ workspaceId: string }>(
    80→      `${baseUrl}/api/workspaces`,
    81→      {
    82→        method: "POST",
    83→        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...user.headers },
    84→        body: JSON.stringify({ name: `Member Test ${Date.now()}` }),
    85→      },
    86→    );
    87→
    88→    const wsRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspace.workspaceId);
    89→    const [members] = await surreal.query<
    90→      [Array<{ in: RecordId; role: string }>]
    91→    >(
    92→      "SELECT in, role FROM member_of WHERE out = $ws LIMIT 1;",
    93→      { ws: wsRecord },

> AGENT

OK so `Bun.serve` uses `config.port` which is `reservedPort`. The race condition: temp server stops on `reservedPort`, then between that and `Bun.serve({ port: reservedPort })`, another process grabs the port. If that happens, `Bun.serve` would either throw or use a different port.

But the setup test PASSES, meaning the server booted and OAuth tokens were obtained successfully. So the port is fine. The remaining tests fail with the token on the same server.

Let me just run these tests locally to see what happens.

> AGENT

Both test files pass locally. These are **CI-only failures** caused by running all smoke tests concurrently. The `--concurrent` flag means multiple test files share the same process, and the module-level JWT validator cache in `auth.ts` gets contaminated across suites.

My `cachedIssuerUrl` fix helps when the URL changes, but with concurrent execution, there's a race: suite A sets the validator for port X, suite B sets it for port Y, then suite A's server gets requests validated against port Y's JWKS URL.

The fix is to **not use module-level state** for the validator. Each server instance should have its own validator. Let me check how `authenticateMcpRequest` is called.

> TOOL

app/src/server/mcp/auth.ts:24:export async function authenticateMcpRequest(
app/src/server/mcp/mcp-route.ts:6:import { authenticateMcpRequest } from "./auth";
app/src/server/mcp/mcp-route.ts:105:    return authenticateMcpRequest(request, workspaceId, surreal, config.betterAuthUrl);
```

> AGENT

The issue is clear. `authenticateMcpRequest` uses module-level `validateToken` and `cachedIssuerUrl`. When `--concurrent` runs multiple test files in the same process, each file's server has a different port/issuerUrl but they all share the same module-level cache. Race condition: validator gets set for one port, then another suite's requests use that stale validator.

The fix: remove the module-level cache and instead accept a validator or use a `Map` keyed by issuerUrl.

> TOOL

  "new_string": "const validatorsByIssuer = new Map<string, (token: string) => Promise<BrainTokenClaims>>();\n\n/**\n * Authenticate an MCP request via OAuth 2.1 JWT Bearer token.\n * Validates the JWT signature via JWKS, extracts claims, and verifies workspace access.\n * Returns auth context on success, or an error Response.\n */\nexport async function authenticateMcpRequest(\n  request: Request,\n  workspaceId: string,\n  surreal: Surreal,\n  issuerUrl: string,\n): Promise<McpAuthResult | Response> {\n  const authHeader = request.headers.get(\"authorization\");\n  if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith(\"Bearer \")) {\n    return jsonError(\"missing or invalid Authorization header\", 401);\n  }\n\n  const token = authHeader.slice(7);\n  if (!token) {\n    return jsonError(\"empty Bearer token\", 401);\n  }\n\n  // Get or create a JWT validator for this issuer URL (safe for concurrent test suites)\n  let validateToken=[REDACTED].get(issuerUrl);\n  if (!validateToken) {\n    validateToken=[REDACTED](issuerUrl);\n    validatorsByIssuer.set(issuerUrl, validateToken);\n  }"
}

    37→export async function bootstrapWorkspaceIdentities(

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/manila/tests/smoke/unified-identity/auth-rewiring.test.ts",
  "old_string": "    const wsRecord = new RecordId(\"workspace\", workspace.workspaceId);\n\n    // Find identity created by bootstrap for this workspace\n    const [identities] = await surreal.query<\n      [Array<{ id: RecordId; type: string; name: string }>]\n    >(\n      \"SELECT id, type, name FROM identity WHERE workspace = $ws AND type = 'human' LIMIT 1;\",\n      { ws: wsRecord },\n    );\n\n    expect(identities.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);",
  "new_string": "    const wsRecord = new RecordId(\"workspace\", workspace.workspaceId);\n\n    // Find identity created by bootstrap for this workspace\n    // Retry briefly in case the server's DB write hasn't propagated to our connection yet\n    let identities: Array<{ id: RecordId; type: string; name: string }> = [];\n    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 10; attempt++) {\n      const [rows] = await surreal.query<\n        [Array<{ id: RecordId; type: string; name: string }>]\n      >(\n        \"SELECT id, type, name FROM identity WHERE workspace = $ws AND type = 'human' LIMIT 1;\",\n        { ws: wsRecord },\n      );\n      identities = rows;\n      if (identities.length > 0) break;\n      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 200));\n    }\n\n    expect(identities.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);"
}

> AGENT

Pushed as `8722224`. The two fixes:

1. **OAuth MCP Auth (4 tests)**: Replaced single module-level `validateToken` + `cachedIssuerUrl` with a `Map<string, validator>` keyed by issuerUrl. When `--concurrent` runs multiple test suites in the same process, each suite's server gets its own validator instead of racing on a shared one.

2. **Auth-rewiring identity test (1 test)**: Added a retry loop (up to 10 × 200ms) for the identity query, handling the case where the server's DB write hasn't propagated to the test's surreal connection under CI load.
